```
1→"""Unused declarations detection via tsc TS6133/TS6192.
     2→
     3→Includes a Deno/edge-functions fallback where `tsc` cannot model URL-based imports.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import argparse
     9→import json
    10→import logging
    11→import re
    12→import subprocess  # nosec B404
    13→from collections import defaultdict
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from shutil import which
    16→
    17→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel, resolve_path, safe_write_text
    18→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    19→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import find_ts_and_tsx_files
    20→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize, print_table
    21→from desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.unused_fallback import (
    22→    _contains_deno_markers,
    23→    _extract_import_names,
    24→    _has_deno_import_syntax,
    25→    _identifier_occurrences,
    26→    detect_unused_fallback,
    27→    should_use_deno_fallback,
    28→)
    29→
    30→TS6133_RE = re.compile(
    31→    r"^(.+)\((\d+),(\d+)\): error TS6133: '(\S+)' is declared but its value is never read\."
    32→)
    33→TS6192_RE = re.compile(
    34→    r"^(.+)\((\d+),(\d+)\): error TS6192: All imports in import declaration are unused\."
    35→)
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→# Compatibility aliases for external callers/tests that imported private names.
    39→_detect_unused_fallback = detect_unused_fallback
    40→_should_use_deno_fallback = should_use_deno_fallback
    41→
    42→
    43→def _resolve_cli_executable(name: str) -> str:
    44→    """Return an absolute CLI path when available."""
    45→    return which(name) or name
    46→
    47→
    48→def detect_unused(path: Path, category: str = "all") -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    49→    ts_files = find_ts_and_tsx_files(path)
    50→    total_files = len(ts_files)
    51→    if _should_use_deno_fallback(path, ts_files):
    52→        return _detect_unused_fallback(path, category)
    53→
    54→    tmp_tsconfig = {
    55→        "extends": "./tsconfig.app.json",
    56→        "compilerOptions": {
    57→            "noUnusedLocals": True,
    58→            "noUnusedParameters": True,
    59→        },
    60→    }
    61→    tmp_path = get_project_root() / "tsconfig.desloppify.json"
    62→    try:
    63→        safe_write_text(tmp_path, json.dumps(tmp_tsconfig, indent=2))
    64→        try:
    65→            # Static tsc argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
    66→            result = subprocess.run(
    67→                [
    68→                    _resolve_cli_executable("npx"),
    69→                    "tsc",
    70→                    "--project",
    71→                    str(tmp_path),
    72→                    "--noEmit",
    73→                ],
    74→                capture_output=True,
    75→                text=True,
    76→                cwd=get_project_root(),
    77→                timeout=120,
    78→                shell=False,  # nosec B603
    79→            )
    80→        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as exc:
    81→            logger.debug("Falling back to source-based unused detection: %s", exc)
    82→            return _detect_unused_fallback(path, category)
    83→    finally:
    84→        tmp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    85→
    86→    entries = []
    87→    for line in result.stdout.splitlines() + result.stderr.splitlines():
    88→        m = TS6133_RE.match(line)
    89→        m2 = TS6192_RE.match(line) if not m else None
    90→        if not m and not m2:
    91→            continue
    92→        if m:
    93→            filepath, lineno, col, name = (
    94→                m.group(1),
    95→                int(m.group(2)),
    96→                int(m.group(3)),
    97→                m.group(4),
    98→            )
    99→            if name.startswith("_"):
   100→                continue
   101→        else:
   102→            filepath, lineno, col = m2.group(1), int(m2.group(2)), int(m2.group(3))
   103→            name = "(entire import)"
   104→
   105→        try:
   106→            full = Path(resolve_path(filepath))
   107→            if not str(full).startswith(str(path.resolve())):
   108→                continue
   109→        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
   110→            logger.debug("Skipping path scope check for %s: %s", filepath, exc)
   111→            continue
   112→
   113→        cat = _categorize_unused(filepath, lineno)
   114→        if category != "all" and cat != category:
   115→            continue
   116→        entries.append(
   117→            {
   118→                "file": filepath,
   119→                "line": lineno,
   120→                "col": col,
   121→                "name": name,
   122→                "category": cat,
   123→            }
   124→        )
   125→    return entries, total_files
   126→
   127→
   128→def _categorize_unused(filepath: str, lineno: int) -> str:
   129→    try:
   130→        p = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
   131→        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
   132→        if lineno <= len(lines):
   133→            src_line = lines[lineno - 1].strip()
   134→            if src_line.startswith("import ") or "from '" in src_line or 'from "' in src_line:
   135→                return "imports"
   136→            if src_line.startswith(
   137→                (
   138→                    "const ",
   139→                    "let ",
   140→                    "var ",
   141→                    "export ",
   142→                    "function ",
   143→                    "class ",
   144→                    "type ",
   145→                    "interface ",
   146→                )
   147→            ):
   148→                return "vars"
   149→            for back in range(1, 10):
   150→                idx = lineno - 1 - back
   151→                if idx < 0:
   152→                    break
   153→                prev = lines[idx].strip()
   154→                if prev.startswith("import "):
   155→                    return "imports"
   156→                if not prev or (
   157→                    not prev.startswith("{") and not prev.startswith(",") and "," not in prev
   158→                ):
   159→                    break
   160→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   161→        logger.debug("Unable to read %s for unused categorization: %s", filepath, exc)
   162→        return "imports"
   163→    return "imports"
   164→
   165→
   166→def cmd_unused(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   167→    path = Path(args.path)
   168→    if _should_use_deno_fallback(path, find_ts_and_tsx_files(path)):
   169→        print(
   170→            colorize(
   171→                "Deno/edge TypeScript context detected — using source-based unused scan",
   172→                "dim",
   173→            ),
   174→            file=sys.stderr,
   175→        )
   176→    else:
   177→        print(colorize("Running tsc... (this may take a moment)", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   178→
   179→    entries, _ = detect_unused(path, args.category)
   180→    if args.json:
   181→        print(json.dumps({"count": len(entries), "entries": entries}, indent=2))
   182→        return
   183→
   184→    if not entries:
   185→        print(colorize("No unused declarations found.", "green"))
   186→        return
   187→
   188→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   189→    for entry in entries:
   190→        by_file[entry["file"]].append(entry)
   191→
   192→    by_cat: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   193→    for entry in entries:
   194→        by_cat[entry["category"]] += 1
   195→
   196→    print(
   197→        colorize(
   198→            f"\nUnused declarations: {len(entries)} across {len(by_file)} files\n",
   199→            "bold",
   200→        )
   201→    )
   202→
   203→    print(colorize("By category:", "cyan"))
   204→    for cat, count in sorted(by_cat.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]):
   205→        print(f"  {cat}: {count}")
   206→    print()
   207→
   208→    print(colorize("Top files:", "cyan"))
   209→    sorted_files = sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
   210→    rows = []
   211→    for filepath, file_entries in sorted_files[: args.top]:
   212→        names = ", ".join(entry["name"] for entry in file_entries[:5])
   213→        if len(file_entries) > 5:
   214→            names += f", ... (+{len(file_entries) - 5})"
   215→        rows.append([rel(filepath), str(len(file_entries)), names])
   216→    print_table(["File", "Count", "Names"], rows, [55, 6, 50])
```

```
1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for `desloppify autofix --dry-run`."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→
    11→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries: list[dict], results: list[dict]) -> None:
    15→    """Print sampled before/after context for fix --dry-run."""
    16→    print(colorize("\n  ── Sample changes (before → after) ──", "cyan"))
    17→    for result in results[:5]:
    18→        _print_fix_file_sample(result, entries)
    19→    removed_count = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    20→    if len(entries) > removed_count:
    21→        print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(entries) - removed_count} of {len(entries)} entries were skipped (complex patterns, rest elements, etc.)", "dim"))
    22→    print()
    23→
    24→
    25→def _print_fix_file_sample(result: dict, entries: list[dict]) -> None:
    26→    filepath, removed_set = result["file"], set(result["removed"])
    27→    try:
    28→        path = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else Path(".") / filepath
    29→        lines = path.read_text().splitlines()
    30→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    31→        _logger.debug("dry-run sample skipped for %s: %s", filepath, exc)
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    file_entries = [entry for entry in entries if entry["file"] == filepath and entry.get("name", "") in removed_set]
    35→    shown = 0
    36→    for entry in file_entries[:2]:
    37→        line_idx = entry.get("line", entry.get("detail", {}).get("line", 0)) - 1
    38→        if line_idx < 0 or line_idx >= len(lines):
    39→            continue
    40→        if shown == 0:
    41→            print(colorize(f"\n  {rel(filepath)}:", "cyan"))
    42→        name = entry.get("name", entry.get("summary", "?"))
    43→        ctx_s, ctx_e = max(0, line_idx - 1), min(len(lines), line_idx + 2)
    44→        print(colorize(f"    {name} (line {line_idx + 1}):", "dim"))
    45→        for idx in range(ctx_s, ctx_e):
    46→            marker = colorize("  →", "red") if idx == line_idx else "   "
    47→            print(f"    {marker} {idx+1:4d}  {lines[idx][:90]}")
    48→        shown += 1
    49→
```

```
   225→        next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   226→            render_context.state,

   228→            issues_scoped,
   229→            strict_score=strict_score,

   235→    print()
   236→

   238→        print_user_message(

   240→            " `desloppify plan resolve`. Full queue:"
   241→            " `desloppify plan show`."
   242→        )
   243→
   244→
   245→def _build_and_render_queue_view(
   246→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   247→    state: dict,
   248→    config: dict,
   249→    *,
   250→    resolve_lang_fn=resolve_lang,
   251→    load_plan_fn=load_plan,
   252→    build_work_queue_fn=build_work_queue,
   253→    write_query_fn=write_query,
   254→    view: QueueViewConfig,
   255→) -> None:
   256→    """Build queue payload and render output for a queue surface."""
   257→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   258→    guardrail_warnings = triage_guardrail_messages(state=state)
   259→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   260→
   261→    plan = load_plan_fn()
   262→    plan_for_queue = plan
   263→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   264→    if view.show_plan_context and (
   265→        plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters")
   266→    ):
   267→        plan_data = plan
   268→
   269→    ctx = queue_context(
   270→        state,
   271→        config=config,
   272→        plan=plan_for_queue,
   273→        target_strict=target_strict,
   274→    )
   275→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   276→        plan_for_queue,
   277→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   278→        scope=opts.scope,
   279→    )
   280→
   281→    queue = build_work_queue_fn(
   282→        state,
   283→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   284→            count=None,
   285→            scope=opts.scope,
   286→            status=opts.status,
   287→            include_subjective=True,
   288→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   289→            explain=opts.explain,
   290→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   291→            context=ctx,
   292→        ),
   293→    )
   294→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   295→    if view.collapse_plan_clusters and effective_cluster and plan_for_queue:
   296→        items = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan_for_queue, effective_cluster)
   297→    elif (
   298→        view.collapse_plan_clusters
   299→        and plan_for_queue
   300→        and not effective_cluster
   301→        and not plan_for_queue.get("active_cluster")
   302→    ):
   303→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan_for_queue)
   304→
   305→    if opts.count:
   306→        items = items[: opts.count]
   307→        queue["items"] = items
   308→        queue["total"] = len(items)
   309→
   310→    render_context = QueueRenderContext(
   311→        queue=queue,
   312→        items=items,
   313→        state=state,
   314→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   315→        plan_data=plan_data,
   316→        ctx=ctx,
   317→        target_strict=target_strict,
   318→        opts=opts,
   319→        command_name=view.command_name,
   320→        show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→    if not items:
   324→        _render_empty_queue_view(
   325→            render_context=render_context,
   326→            guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   327→            write_query_fn=write_query_fn,
   328→        )
   329→        return
   330→
   331→    lang = resolve_lang_fn(args)
   332→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   333→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   334→        state,
   335→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command=view.command_name, plan=plan_data),
   336→    )
   337→    payload = _write_next_payload(
   338→        queue=queue,
   339→        items=items,
   340→        state=state,
   341→        narrative=narrative,
   342→        plan_data=plan_data,
   343→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   344→        write_query_fn=write_query_fn,
   345→        command_name=view.command_name,
   346→    )
   347→
   348→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items, command_name=view.command_name):
   349→        return
   350→
   351→    _render_terminal_queue_view(
   352→        render_context=render_context,
   353→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   354→        show_execution_prompt=view.show_execution_prompt,
   355→    )
   356→
   357→

   359→    args: argparse.Namespace,
```

```
    41→def scorecard_subjective(
    42→    state: dict,
    43→    dim_scores: dict,
    44→) -> list[dict]:
    45→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    46→    if not dim_scores:
    47→        return []
    48→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    49→        state,
    50→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    51→    )
    52→
    53→
    54→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    55→    issues_scoped: dict,
    56→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    57→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    58→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(issues_scoped)
    59→
    60→
    61→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    62→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    63→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify autofix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    64→
    65→
    66→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    67→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    68→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    69→        return "[review]"
    70→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    71→        return "[auto]"
    72→    return ""
    73→
    74→
    75→def render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    76→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    77→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    78→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    79→        for item in grouped_items:
    80→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    81→            tag = effort_tag(item)
    82→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    83→            print(
    84→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    85→            )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _cluster_type_label(cluster_name: str, action_type: str) -> str:
    89→    if cluster_name in CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names:
    90→        return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names[cluster_name]
    91→    return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.action_types.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    92→
    93→
    94→def _render_cluster_files(members: list[dict]) -> None:
    95→    file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
    96→    if len(file_counts) <= 5:
    97→        print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
    98→        for filename, count in file_counts.most_common():
    99→            print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   103→    for filename, count in file_counts.most_common(3):
   104→        print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   105→    remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   106→    print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _render_cluster_sample(members: list[dict]) -> None:
   110→    print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   111→    for member in members[:3]:
   112→        summary = member.get("summary") or member.get("id", "")
   113→        print(f"    - {summary}")
   114→    if len(members) > 3:
   115→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   116→
   117→
   118→def cluster_action_commands(cluster_name: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   119→    """Return semantic cluster action commands independent of terminal labels."""
   120→    return {
   121→        "resolve_all": f'desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   122→        "drill_in": f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   123→        "skip": f"desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}",
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   128→    commands = cluster_action_commands(cluster_name)
   129→    print(colorize(f"\n  Skip:          {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   130→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      {commands['drill_in']}", "dim"))
   131→    print(
   132→        colorize(
   133→            f"  Resolve all:   {commands['resolve_all']}",
   134→            "dim",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   140→    commands = cluster_action_commands(cluster_name)
   141→    print(
   142→        colorize(
   143→            f"\n  Resolve all:   {commands['resolve_all']}",
   144→            "dim",
   145→        )
   146→    )
   147→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      {commands['drill_in']}", "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   149→
   150→
   151→def _step_display_text(step: str | dict) -> str:
   152→    """Extract display text from an action step (string or dict with title)."""
   153→    if isinstance(step, dict):
   154→        return step.get("title", str(step))
   155→    return str(step)
   156→
   157→
   158→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   159→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   160→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   161→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   162→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   163→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   164→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(cluster_name, action_type)
   165→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   166→    done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))
   167→    step_badge = f" [{done_count}/{len(action_steps)} steps done]" if action_steps else ""
   168→    optional_tag = " — optional" if is_optional else ""
   169→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{step_badge}{optional_tag})", "bold"))
   170→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   171→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   172→
   173→    # Action steps
   174→    if action_steps:
   175→        show_count = min(3, len(action_steps))
   176→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps[:show_count], 1):
   177→            marker = "[x]" if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("done") else "[ ]"
   178→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
   179→        remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count
   180→        if remaining > 0:
   181→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more — drill in to view all", "dim"))
   182→
   183→    dep_order = item.get("dependency_order")
   184→    if dep_order is not None and dep_order <= 2:
   185→        print(colorize("  Priority: complete before other clusters", "cyan"))
   186→
   187→    # Cluster members
   188→    members = item.get("members", [])
   189→    if members:
   190→        _render_cluster_files(members)
   191→        _render_cluster_sample(members)
   192→
   193→    # Primary action
   194→    autofix_hint = item.get("autofix_hint")
   195→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   196→    if autofix_hint:
   197→        print(colorize(f"\n  Try auto first: {autofix_hint}", "cyan"))
   198→        print(colorize("  If auto finds 0, drill into individual issues:", "dim"))
   199→    if primary_command:
   200→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   201→
   202→    if is_optional:
   203→        _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   204→        return
   205→
   206→    _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   207→
   208→
   209→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   210→    del explain
   211→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   212→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   213→    # When the only item is the "run scan" workflow action, show "Queue cleared"
   214→    # instead of a misleading "1 items" count.
   215→    if (
   216→        total == 1
   217→        and len(items) == 1
   218→        and items[0].get("id") == "workflow::run-scan"
   219→    ):
   220→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared (1 workflow step)", "bold"))
   221→    else:
   222→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   223→        if total > 5:
   224→            print(colorize("  (Skip items only when explicitly requested.)", "dim"))
   225→
   226→
   227→def show_empty_queue(
   228→    queue: dict,
   229→    strict: float | None,
   230→    *,
   231→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   232→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   233→) -> bool:
   234→    del target_strict
   235→    if queue.get("items"):
   236→        return False
   237→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   238→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   239→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   240→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   241→        print(colorize(
   242→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   243→            "cyan",
   244→        ))
   245→        print(colorize(
   246→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   247→            "dim",
   248→        ))
   249→        return True
   250→
   251→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   252→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   253→    return True
   254→
   255→
   256→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   257→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   258→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   259→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   260→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   261→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   262→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) and (plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   263→        tag_str += " [plan]"
   264→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   265→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   266→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   267→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   268→
   269→
   270→__all__ = [
   271→    "CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS",
   272→    "ClusterTypeLabels",
   273→    "cluster_action_commands",
   274→    "effort_tag",
   275→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   276→    "render_cluster_item",
   277→    "render_compact_item",
   278→    "render_grouped",
   279→    "render_queue_header",
   280→    "scorecard_subjective",
   281→    "show_empty_queue",
   282→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   283→]
   284→
```

```
    28→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    29→    if len(text) <= width:
    30→        return text
    31→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    32→
    33→
    34→def _print_queue_header(
    35→    *,
    36→    items: list[dict],
    37→    include_skipped: bool,
    38→    plan: dict,
    39→    new_count: int = 0,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    total = len(items)
    42→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    43→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    44→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    45→
    46→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    47→    subjective = sum(
    48→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    49→    )
    50→
    51→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    52→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    53→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    54→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    55→
    56→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    57→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    58→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    59→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    60→        subjective=subjective,
    61→    )
    62→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    63→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    64→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    65→
    66→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster")
    67→    if focus:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    69→
    70→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    71→        return
    72→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    73→        return
    74→    print(
    75→        colorize(
    76→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    77→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )

    83→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:

    95→def _render_cluster_banner(item: dict, position: int, new_ids: set[str]) -> None:
    96→    """Render a collapsed cluster as a visually distinct banner."""
    97→    name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
    98→    member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
    99→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   100→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(name, action_type)
   101→    cluster_type_tag = "[auto]" if item.get("cluster_auto", True) else "[triage]"
   102→    new_in_cluster = sum(
   103→        1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   104→        if m.get("id") in new_ids
   105→    )
   106→    new_tag = f"  (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   107→    # Step progress badge
   108→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   109→    step_badge = ""
   110→    if action_steps:
   111→        done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))
   112→        step_badge = f"  [{done_count}/{len(action_steps)} steps]"
   113→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   114→    command = item.get("primary_command", "")
   115→
   116→    label = f"{position}. {cluster_type_tag} {type_label} — {member_count} items{step_badge}{new_tag}"
   117→    width = max(len(label) + 4, 50)
   118→    bar = "─" * width
   119→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "dim"))
   120→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "bold"))
   121→    print(colorize(f"     Cluster: {name}", "dim"))
   122→    if summary:
   123→        print(colorize(f"     {summary}", "dim"))
   124→    if command:
   125→        print(colorize(f"     Run:      {command}", "cyan"))
   126→    print(colorize(f"     Drill in: desloppify plan queue --cluster {name}", "dim"))
   127→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "dim"))
   128→
   129→
   130→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
   131→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   132→    _new = new_ids or set()
   133→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   134→        pos = str(idx)
   135→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   136→
   137→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   138→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   139→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   140→            conf_str = "—"
   141→            detector = "planning"
   142→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   143→            cluster_name = ""
   144→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   145→            conf_str = "—"
   146→            detector = "workflow"
   147→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   148→            cluster_name = ""
   149→        elif kind == "cluster":
   150→            # Clusters are rendered as banners, not table rows
   151→            continue
   152→        else:
   153→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   154→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   156→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   157→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   158→
   159→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   160→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   161→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   162→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   163→    return rows
   164→
   165→
   166→def _resolve_effective_cluster(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> str | None:
   167→    """Resolve explicit cluster filter or fallback to active cluster focus."""
   168→    if cluster_filter:
   169→        return cluster_filter
   170→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   171→    if active_cluster:
   172→        return active_cluster
   173→    return None
   174→
   175→
   176→def _build_queue_items(
   177→    *,
   178→    state: dict,
   179→    plan: dict,
   180→    include_skipped: bool,
   181→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   182→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   183→    """Build queue items with focus/collapse view transforms applied."""
   184→    queue = build_execution_queue(
   185→        state,
   186→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   187→            count=None,
   188→            status="open",
   189→            include_subjective=True,
   190→            plan=plan,
   191→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   192→        ),
   193→    )
   194→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   195→    if effective_cluster:
   196→        items = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, effective_cluster)
   197→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   198→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   199→    return items, queue
   200→
   201→
   202→def _compute_visible_new_ids(*, queue: dict, plan: dict, state: dict, items: list[dict]) -> set[str]:
   203→    """Compute new IDs for the current queue view, including review-driven IDs."""
   204→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   205→    all_new_ids |= compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   206→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   207→    return all_new_ids & item_ids
   208→
   209→
   210→def _render_cluster_banners(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str]) -> int:
   211→    """Render collapsed cluster banners and return banner count."""
   212→    cluster_count = 0
   213→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   214→        if item.get("kind") != "cluster":
   215→            continue
   216→        _render_cluster_banner(item, idx, new_ids)
   217→        cluster_count += 1
   218→    return cluster_count
   219→
   220→
   221→def _render_queue_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
   222→    """Render queue rows table. Returns True when rows were printed."""
   223→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   224→    if not rows:
   225→        return False
   226→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   227→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   228→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   229→    return True
   230→
   231→
   232→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   233→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   234→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   235→    state = runtime.state
   236→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   240→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   241→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   242→
   243→    plan = load_plan()
   244→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   245→
   246→    effective_cluster = _resolve_effective_cluster(plan, cluster_filter)
   247→    items, queue = _build_queue_items(
   248→        state=state,
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   251→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   255→    new_ids = _compute_visible_new_ids(queue=queue, plan=plan, state=state, items=items)
   256→
   257→    if sort_by == "recent":
   258→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   259→
   260→    _print_queue_header(
   261→        items=items,
   262→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   263→        plan=plan,
   264→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   265→    )
   266→
   267→    if not items:
   268→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   269→        return
   270→
   271→    # Determine which items to show
   272→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   273→
   274→    print()
   275→    cluster_count = _render_cluster_banners(display_items, new_ids)
   276→    rows_rendered = _render_queue_rows(display_items, new_ids)
   277→
   278→    if cluster_count and rows_rendered:
   279→        print(colorize(
   280→            f"  ({cluster_count} cluster banner{'s' if cluster_count != 1 else ''}"
   281→            " shown above — table shows remaining individual items)",
   282→            "dim",
   283→        ))
   284→
   285→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   286→        remaining = len(items) - top
   287→        print(colorize(
```

```
1→"""Read-only git observation with graceful degradation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import subprocess  # nosec B404
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from shutil import which
     9→
    10→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    11→
    12→_GIT_TIMEOUT = 5
    13→
    14→
    15→def _resolve_cli_executable(name: str) -> str:
    16→    """Return an absolute CLI path when available."""
    17→    return which(name) or name
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    21→class GitContext:
    22→    available: bool
    23→    branch: str | None = None
    24→    head_sha: str | None = None
    25→    has_uncommitted: bool = False
    26→    root: str | None = None
    27→
    28→
    29→def detect_git_context() -> GitContext:
    30→    """Detect current git context (branch, HEAD, uncommitted changes).
    31→
    32→    Returns ``available=False`` when git is missing or not in a repo.
    33→    """
    34→    git_cli = _resolve_cli_executable("git")
    35→    try:
    36→        # Static git argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
    37→        head = subprocess.run(
    38→            [git_cli, "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
    39→            capture_output=True,
    40→            text=True,
    41→            timeout=_GIT_TIMEOUT,
    42→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    43→        )
    44→        if head.returncode != 0:
    45→            return GitContext(available=False)
    46→
    47→        sha = head.stdout.strip()[:12]
    48→
    49→        branch_result = subprocess.run(
    50→            [git_cli, "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"],
    51→            capture_output=True,
    52→            text=True,
    53→            timeout=_GIT_TIMEOUT,
    54→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    55→        )
    56→        branch = branch_result.stdout.strip() if branch_result.returncode == 0 else None
    57→
    58→        root_result = subprocess.run(
    59→            [git_cli, "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"],
    60→            capture_output=True,
    61→            text=True,
    62→            timeout=_GIT_TIMEOUT,
    63→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    64→        )
    65→        root = root_result.stdout.strip() if root_result.returncode == 0 else None
    66→
    67→        status_result = subprocess.run(
    68→            [git_cli, "status", "--porcelain"],
    69→            capture_output=True,
    70→            text=True,
    71→            timeout=_GIT_TIMEOUT,
    72→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    73→        )
    74→        has_uncommitted = bool(status_result.stdout.strip()) if status_result.returncode == 0 else False
    75→
    76→        return GitContext(
    77→            available=True,
    78→            branch=branch,
    79→            head_sha=sha,
    80→            has_uncommitted=has_uncommitted,
    81→            root=root,
    82→        )
    83→    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired, OSError) as exc:
    84→        logger.debug("git context unavailable: %s", exc)
    85→        return GitContext(available=False)
    86→
    87→
    88→def update_pr_body(pr_number: int, body: str) -> bool:
    89→    """Update PR description via ``gh pr edit``.  Returns True on success."""
    90→    gh_cli = _resolve_cli_executable("gh")
    91→    try:
    92→        # Static gh argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
    93→        result = subprocess.run(
    94→            [gh_cli, "pr", "edit", str(pr_number), "--body", body],
    95→            capture_output=True,
    96→            text=True,
    97→            timeout=_GIT_TIMEOUT * 3,
    98→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    99→        )
   100→        if result.returncode != 0:
   101→            logger.warning("gh pr edit failed: %s", result.stderr.strip())
   102→            return False
   103→        return True
   104→    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired, OSError) as exc:
   105→        logger.warning("gh pr edit unavailable: %s", exc)
   106→        return False
   107→
   108→
   109→__all__ = ["GitContext", "detect_git_context", "update_pr_body"]
   110→
```

```
1→"""Metadata helpers for scorecard rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import re
     7→import subprocess  # nosec B404
     8→from collections.abc import Callable
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from shutil import which
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _resolve_cli_executable(name: str) -> str:
    18→    """Return an absolute CLI path when available."""
    19→    return which(name) or name
    20→
    21→
    22→def resolve_project_name(project_root: Path) -> str:
    23→    """Resolve owner/repo display name from GitHub CLI, git remote, or folder."""
    24→    gh_cli = _resolve_cli_executable("gh")
    25→    try:
    26→        # Static gh argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
    27→        name = subprocess.check_output(
    28→            [
    29→                gh_cli,
    30→                "repo",
    31→                "view",
    32→                "--json",
    33→                "nameWithOwner",
    34→                "-q",
    35→                ".nameWithOwner",
    36→            ],
    37→            cwd=str(project_root),
    38→            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    39→            text=True,
    40→            timeout=5,
    41→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    42→        ).strip()
    43→        if "/" in name:
    44→            return name
    45→    except (
    46→        subprocess.CalledProcessError,
    47→        FileNotFoundError,
    48→        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    49→    ) as exc:
    50→        logger.debug("gh repo view failed, falling back to git remote: %s", exc)
    51→
    52→    try:
    53→        git_cli = _resolve_cli_executable("git")
    54→        # Static git argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
    55→        url = subprocess.check_output(
    56→            [git_cli, "config", "--get", "remote.origin.url"],
    57→            cwd=str(project_root),
    58→            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    59→            text=True,
    60→            timeout=5,
    61→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    62→        ).strip()
    63→        if url.startswith("git@") and ":" in url:
    64→            path = url.split(":")[-1]
    65→        else:
    66→            path = "/".join(url.split("/")[-2:])
    67→        return path.removesuffix(".git")
    68→    except (
    69→        subprocess.CalledProcessError,
    70→        FileNotFoundError,
    71→        IndexError,
    72→        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    73→    ) as exc:
    74→        logger.debug("git remote lookup failed, falling back to folder name: %s", exc)
    75→        return project_root.name
    76→
    77→
    78→def resolve_package_version(
    79→    project_root: Path,
    80→    *,
    81→    version_getter: Callable[[str], str],
    82→    package_not_found_error: type[Exception],
    83→) -> str:
    84→    """Resolve package version from installed metadata or local pyproject."""
    85→    try:
    86→        return version_getter("desloppify")
    87→    except package_not_found_error as exc:
    88→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "read package metadata version", exc)
    89→
    90→    pyproject_path = project_root / "pyproject.toml"
    91→    try:
    92→        text = pyproject_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    93→        match = re.search(r'^\s*version\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"\s*$', text, re.MULTILINE)
    94→        if match:
    95→            return match.group(1)
    96→    except OSError as exc:
    97→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "read pyproject.toml for package version", exc)
    98→
    99→    return "unknown"
   100→
```

```
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel

    19→def _resolve_fixer_results(
    20→    state: dict, results: list[dict], detector: str, fixer_name: str
    21→) -> list[str]:
    22→    resolved_ids = []
    23→    for result in results:
    24→        result_file = rel(result["file"])
    25→        for symbol in result["removed"]:
    26→            issue_id = f"{detector}::{result_file}::{symbol}"
    27→            if issue_id in state["issues"] and state["issues"][issue_id]["status"] == "open":
    28→                state["issues"][issue_id]["status"] = "fixed"
    29→                state["issues"][issue_id]["note"] = (
    30→                    f"auto-fixed by desloppify autofix {fixer_name}"
    31→                )
    32→                resolved_ids.append(issue_id)
    33→    return resolved_ids

    36→def _warn_uncommitted_changes() -> None:

    58→                    "dim",

    60→            )
    61→    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
    62→        return
    63→
    64→
    65→def _cascade_unused_import_cleanup(
    66→    path: Path,
    67→    state: dict,
    68→    _prev_score: float,
    69→    dry_run: bool,
    70→    *,
    71→    lang: LangRun | None = None,
    72→) -> None:
    73→    if not lang or "unused-imports" not in getattr(lang, "fixers", {}):
    74→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no unused-imports fixer for this language", "dim"))
    75→        return
    76→
    77→    fixer = lang.fixers["unused-imports"]
    78→    print(colorize("\n  Running cascading import cleanup...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    79→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
    80→    if not entries:
    81→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
    82→        return
    83→
    84→    raw_results = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    85→    results = raw_results.entries if isinstance(raw_results, FixResult) else raw_results
    86→
    87→    if not results:
    88→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    removed_count = sum(len(result["removed"]) for result in results)
    92→    removed_lines = sum(result["lines_removed"] for result in results)
    93→    print(
    94→        colorize(
    95→            f"  Cascade: removed {removed_count} now-orphaned imports "
    96→            f"from {len(results)} files ({removed_lines} lines)",
    97→            "green",
    98→        )
    99→    )
   100→    resolved = _resolve_fixer_results(
   101→        state, results, fixer.detector, "cascade-unused-imports"
   102→    )
   103→    if resolved:
   104→        print(f"  Cascade: auto-resolved {len(resolved)} import issues")
   105→
   106→
   107→_SKIP_REASON_LABELS = {
   108→    "rest_element": "has ...rest (removing changes rest contents)",
   109→    "array_destructuring": "array destructuring (positional — can't remove)",
   110→    "function_param": "function/callback parameter (use `fix unused-params` to prefix with _)",
   111→    "standalone_var_with_call": "standalone variable with function call (may have side effects)",
   112→    "no_destr_context": "destructuring member without context",
   113→    "out_of_range": "line out of range (stale data?)",
   114→    "other": "other patterns (needs manual review)",
   115→}
   116→
   117→
   118→def _print_fix_retro(
   119→    fixer_name: str,
   120→    detected: int,
   121→    fixed: int,
   122→    resolved: int,
   123→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   124→):
   125→    skipped = detected - fixed
   126→    print(colorize("\n  ── Post-fix check ──", "dim"))
   127→    print(
   128→        colorize(
   129→            f"  Fixed {fixed}/{detected} ({skipped} skipped, {resolved} issues resolved)",
   130→            "dim",
   131→        )
   132→    )
   133→    if skip_reasons and skipped > 0:
   134→        print(colorize(f"\n  Skip reasons ({skipped} total):", "dim"))
   135→        for reason, count in sorted(skip_reasons.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]):
   136→            print(
   137→                colorize(
   138→                    f"    {count:4d}  {_SKIP_REASON_LABELS.get(reason, reason)}", "dim"
   139→                )
   140→            )
   141→        print()
   142→
   143→    checklist = [
   144→        "Run your language typecheck/build command — does it still build?",
   145→        "Spot-check a few changed files — do the edits look correct?",
   146→    ]
   147→    if skipped > 0 and not skip_reasons:
   148→        checklist.append(
   149→            f"{skipped} items were skipped. Should the fixer handle more patterns?"
   150→        )
   151→    checklist += [
   152→        "Run `desloppify scan` to update state and refresh issues.",
   153→        "Are there cascading effects? (e.g., removing vars may orphan imports)",
   154→        "`git diff --stat` — review before committing. Anything surprising?",
   155→    ]
   156→    print(colorize("  Checklist:", "dim"))
   157→    for index, item in enumerate(checklist, 1):
   158→        print(colorize(f"  {index}. {item}", "dim"))
   159→
   160→
   161→__all__ = [
   162→    "_cascade_unused_import_cleanup",
   163→    "_print_fix_retro",
   164→    "_resolve_fixer_results",
   165→    "_warn_uncommitted_changes",
   166→]
   167→
```

```
1→"""Detect repeated code blocks via jscpd.
     2→
     3→Replaces boilerplate_duplication.py with a thin adapter around jscpd
     4→(https://github.com/kucherenko/jscpd), which uses proper per-language
     5→tokenisation and supports type-2 clones (renamed identifiers).
     6→
     7→Falls back gracefully to None when jscpd/npx is not installed.
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from __future__ import annotations
    11→
    12→import hashlib
    13→import json
    14→import logging
    15→import shutil
    16→import subprocess  # nosec B404
    17→import tempfile
    18→from pathlib import Path
    19→
    20→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import (
    21→
    22→    collect_exclude_dirs,
    23→
    24→    get_exclusions,
    25→
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→_BASE_IGNORES = (
    32→    "**/node_modules/**",
    33→    "**/.git/**",
    34→    "**/__pycache__/**",
    35→    "**/.venv*/**",
    36→    "**/venv/**",
    37→    "**/.desloppify/**",
    38→    "**/.claude/**",
    39→)
    40→
    41→_ARTIFACT_PREFIXES = ("build/", "dist/", ".desloppify/", ".claude/")
    42→_BUILD_MIRROR_PREFIX = "build/lib/"
    43→
    44→
    45→def _to_scan_relative(path_resolved: Path, name: str) -> str | None:
    46→    """Return scan-relative path, or None when file is outside scan path."""
    47→    if not name:
    48→        return None
    49→    try:
    50→        rel = Path(name).resolve().relative_to(path_resolved)
    51→    except (ValueError, OSError):
    52→        return None
    53→    return str(rel).replace("\\", "/")
    54→
    55→
    56→def _is_artifact_path(rel_path: str) -> bool:
    57→    return rel_path.startswith(_ARTIFACT_PREFIXES)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _is_build_mirror_pair(first_rel: str, second_rel: str) -> bool:
    61→    if first_rel.startswith(_BUILD_MIRROR_PREFIX):
    62→        return first_rel[len(_BUILD_MIRROR_PREFIX):] == second_rel
    63→    if second_rel.startswith(_BUILD_MIRROR_PREFIX):
    64→        return second_rel[len(_BUILD_MIRROR_PREFIX):] == first_rel
    65→    return False
    66→
    67→
    68→def _as_jscpd_globs(pattern: str) -> list[str]:
    69→    raw = pattern.strip().replace("\\", "/").strip("/")
    70→    if not raw:
    71→        return []
    72→    globs = [f"**/{raw}/**"]
    73→    basename = raw.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
    74→    if "." in basename or "*" in basename:
    75→        globs.append(f"**/{raw}")
    76→    return globs
    77→
    78→
    79→def _jscpd_ignore_arg(scan_path: Path) -> str:
    80→    """Build jscpd ignore globs from defaults + runtime excludes."""
    81→    patterns = set(_BASE_IGNORES)
    82→    # Use collect_exclude_dirs for the directory-name portion, then convert
    83→    # the remaining runtime globs (patterns with '*') to jscpd format.
    84→    for dirname in collect_exclude_dirs(scan_path):
    85→        base = Path(dirname).name
    86→        patterns.update(_as_jscpd_globs(base))
    87→    for pattern in get_exclusions():
    88→        if "*" in pattern:
    89→            patterns.update(_as_jscpd_globs(pattern))
    90→    return ",".join(sorted(patterns))
    91→
    92→
    93→def _parse_jscpd_report(report: dict, scan_path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    94→    """Parse a jscpd JSON report dict and return clustered duplication entries.
    95→
    96→    Clusters pairwise duplicates that share the same fragment into multi-file
    97→    groups, producing the same output shape as the old boilerplate_duplication
    98→    detector so that phase_boilerplate_duplication() needs no further changes.
    99→    """
   100→    duplicates = report.get("duplicates", [])
   101→    if not duplicates:
   102→        return []
   103→
   104→    path_resolved = scan_path.resolve()
   105→
   106→    # Cluster pairs by SHA256(fragment.strip()[:200])[:16]
   107→    clusters: dict[str, dict] = {}
   108→
   109→    for dup in duplicates:
   110→        fragment = dup.get("fragment", "")
   111→        fragment_key = hashlib.sha256(
   112→            fragment.strip()[:200].encode("utf-8", errors="replace")
   113→        ).hexdigest()[:16]
   114→
   115→        first = dup.get("firstFile", {})
   116→        second = dup.get("secondFile", {})
   117→        first_name = first.get("name", "")
   118→        second_name = second.get("name", "")
   119→
   120→        first_rel = _to_scan_relative(path_resolved, first_name)
   121→        second_rel = _to_scan_relative(path_resolved, second_name)
   122→        if first_rel is None or second_rel is None:
   123→            continue
   124→        if (
   125→            first_rel == second_rel
   126→            or _is_artifact_path(first_rel)
   127→            or _is_artifact_path(second_rel)
   128→            or _is_build_mirror_pair(first_rel, second_rel)
   129→        ):
   130→            continue
   131→
   132→        lines = dup.get("lines", 0)
   133→        if fragment_key not in clusters:
   134→            clusters[fragment_key] = {
   135→                "id": fragment_key,
   136→                "lines": lines,
   137→                "fragment": fragment,
   138→                "files": {},
   139→            }
   140→
   141→        cluster = clusters[fragment_key]
   142→        for name, info in [(first_rel, first), (second_rel, second)]:
   143→            if name not in cluster["files"]:
   144→                cluster["files"][name] = info.get("start", 0)
   145→
   146→    entries: list[dict] = []
   147→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   148→        files = cluster["files"]
   149→        if len(files) < 2:
   150→            continue
   151→        locations = [
   152→            {"file": f, "line": line}
   153→            for f, line in sorted(files.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[0])
   154→        ]
   155→        entries.append(
   156→            {
   157→                "id": cluster["id"],
   158→                "distinct_files": len(files),
   159→                "window_size": cluster["lines"],
   160→                "locations": locations,
   161→                "sample": cluster["fragment"].splitlines()[:4],
   162→            }
   163→        )
   164→
   165→    entries.sort(key=lambda e: (-e["distinct_files"], e["id"]))
   166→    return entries
   167→
   168→
   169→def _resolve_jscpd_command() -> list[str] | None:
   170→    """Return the best available command prefix for running jscpd.
   171→
   172→    Prefers a locally/globally installed ``jscpd`` executable over ``npx``.
   173→    Returns ``None`` when neither ``jscpd`` nor ``npx`` can be found.
   174→    """
   175→    jscpd_path = shutil.which("jscpd")
   176→    if jscpd_path:
   177→        logger.debug("jscpd: using installed executable at %s", jscpd_path)
   178→        return [jscpd_path]
   179→    npx_path = shutil.which("npx")
   180→    if npx_path:
   181→        logger.debug("jscpd: falling back to npx at %s", npx_path)
   182→        return [npx_path, "--yes", "jscpd"]
   183→    return None
   184→
   185→
   186→def detect_with_jscpd(path: Path) -> list[dict] | None:
   187→    """Run jscpd on *path* and return duplication entries, or None on failure."""
   188→    cmd_prefix = _resolve_jscpd_command()
   189→    if cmd_prefix is None:
   190→        warn_best_effort(
   191→            "Boilerplate duplication detection skipped: jscpd/npx not found. "
   192→            "Install with `npm install -g jscpd`."
   193→        )
   194→        logger.debug("jscpd: neither jscpd nor npx found — skipping")
   195→        return None
   196→
   197→    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
   198→        try:
   199→            # Static jscpd argv only; command prefix is resolved before execution.
   200→            subprocess.run(
   201→                [
   202→                    *cmd_prefix,
   203→                    str(path),
   204→                    "--reporters",
   205→                    "json",
   206→                    "--output",
   207→                    tmpdir,
   208→                    "--min-lines",
   209→                    "4",
   210→                    "--min-tokens",
   211→                    "50",
   212→                    "--ignore",
   213→                    _jscpd_ignore_arg(path),
   214→                    "--silent",
   215→                ],
   216→                capture_output=True,
   217→                text=True,
   218→                timeout=120,
   219→                check=True,
   220→                shell=False,  # nosec B603
   221→            )
   222→        except FileNotFoundError:
   223→            warn_best_effort(
   224→                "Boilerplate duplication detection skipped: jscpd/npx not found. "
   225→                "Install with `npm install -g jscpd`."
   226→            )
   227→            logger.debug("jscpd: command not found at runtime — skipping")
   228→            return None
   229→        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
   230→            warn_best_effort(
   231→                "Boilerplate duplication detection skipped: jscpd exited with errors."
   232→            )
   233→            logger.debug(
   234→                "jscpd: non-zero exit (%s): %s",
   235→                exc.returncode,
   236→                (exc.stderr or "").strip(),
   237→            )
   238→            return None
   239→        except OSError as exc:
   240→            warn_best_effort(
   241→                "Boilerplate duplication detection skipped: jscpd failed to run."
   242→            )
   243→            logger.debug("jscpd: OS error running jscpd: %s", exc)
   244→            return None
   245→        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   246→            warn_best_effort(
   247→                "Boilerplate duplication detection skipped: jscpd timed out after 120s."
   248→            )
   249→            logger.debug("jscpd: timed out")
   250→            return None
   251→
   252→        report_file = Path(tmpdir) / "jscpd-report.json"
   253→        if not report_file.exists():
   254→            logger.debug("jscpd: no report file produced — assuming no duplicates")
   255→            return []
   256→
   257→        try:
   258→            report = json.loads(report_file.read_text())
   259→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   260→            logger.debug("jscpd: failed to parse report: %s", exc)
   261→            return None
   262→
   263→        return _parse_jscpd_report(report, path)
   264→
   265→
   266→__all__ = ["_parse_jscpd_report", "_resolve_jscpd_command", "detect_with_jscpd"]
   267→
```

```
1→"""Detect architectural layer violations via import-linter.
     2→
     3→Reads `.importlinter` config from the scanned project's root (walking up
     4→from the scan path to find the first `.importlinter` file, stopping at `.git`
     5→boundaries). Falls back gracefully to None when lint-imports is not installed
     6→or no config is found.
     7→
     8→See https://import-linter.readthedocs.io for config format.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import re
    15→import subprocess  # nosec B404
    16→from pathlib import Path
    17→from shutil import which
    18→
    19→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→_BROKEN_CONTRACT_RE = re.compile(r"Broken contract '([^']+)'")
    22→# import-linter outputs violations as "  module.a imports module.b"
    23→_VIOLATION_IMPORTS_RE = re.compile(r"^\s+(\S+)\s+imports\s+(\S+)\s*$")
    24→# Also handle "  module.a -> module.b" format (arrow notation)
    25→_VIOLATION_ARROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\s+(\S+)\s+->\s+(\S+)\s*$")
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_cli_executable(name: str) -> str:
    29→    """Return an absolute CLI path when available."""
    30→    return which(name) or name
    31→
    32→
    33→def _find_importlinter_root(path: Path) -> Path | None:
    34→    """Walk up from path to find the directory containing .importlinter.
    35→
    36→    Stops when a .git boundary or the filesystem root is encountered.
    37→    """
    38→    candidate = path.resolve()
    39→    for directory in [candidate, *candidate.parents]:
    40→        if (directory / ".importlinter").exists():
    41→            return directory
    42→        # Stop at project root boundaries
    43→        if (directory / ".git").exists() or directory.parent == directory:
    44→            break
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def _module_to_file(module: str) -> str:
    49→    """Convert dotted module name to a relative file path."""
    50→    return module.replace(".", "/") + ".py"
    51→
    52→
    53→def detect_with_import_linter(path: Path) -> list[dict] | None:
    54→    """Run lint-imports and return layer violation entries, or None on failure."""
    55→    config_dir = _find_importlinter_root(path)
    56→    if config_dir is None:
    57→        logger.debug("import-linter: no .importlinter config found — skipping")
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        # Static lint-imports argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
    62→        result = subprocess.run(
    63→            [_resolve_cli_executable("lint-imports")],
    64→            capture_output=True,
    65→            text=True,
    66→            cwd=config_dir,
    67→            timeout=60,
    68→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
    69→        )
    70→    except FileNotFoundError:
    71→        logger.debug("import-linter: lint-imports not found — skipping")
    72→        return None
    73→    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    74→        logger.debug("import-linter: timed out")
    75→        return None
    76→
    77→    if result.returncode == 0:
    78→        return []
    79→
    80→    output = result.stdout + result.stderr
    81→    if not output.strip():
    82→        return []
    83→
    84→    entries: list[dict] = []
    85→    current_contract: str | None = None
    86→
    87→    for line in output.splitlines():
    88→        m_contract = _BROKEN_CONTRACT_RE.search(line)
    89→        if m_contract:
    90→            current_contract = m_contract.group(1)
    91→            continue
    92→
    93→        if current_contract is None:
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        m_viol = _VIOLATION_IMPORTS_RE.match(line) or _VIOLATION_ARROW_RE.match(line)
    97→        if not m_viol:
    98→            continue
    99→
   100→        source_mod = m_viol.group(1)
   101→        target_mod = m_viol.group(2)
   102→        source_file = _module_to_file(source_mod)
   103→        source_pkg = source_mod.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] if "." in source_mod else source_mod
   104→
   105→        entries.append(
   106→            {
   107→                "file": source_file,
   108→                "line": 0,
   109→                "summary": (
   110→                    f"Layer violation: {source_mod} -> {target_mod}"
   111→                    f" (contract: {current_contract})"
   112→                ),
   113→                "confidence": "high",
   114→                "source_pkg": source_pkg,
   115→                "target_pkg": target_mod,
   116→            }
   117→        )
   118→
   119→    return entries
   120→
   121→
   122→__all__ = ["detect_with_import_linter"]
   123→
```

```
1→"""Python unused detection via ruff (F401=unused imports, F841=unused vars)."""
     2→
     3→import json
     4→import re
     5→import subprocess  # nosec B404
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from shutil import which
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import collect_exclude_dirs as _collect_exclude_dirs
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import find_py_files
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import get_exclusions as _get_exclusions
    12→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import matches_exclusion as _matches_exclusion
    13→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    14→
    15→
    16→def _resolve_cli_executable(name: str) -> str:
    17→    """Return an absolute CLI path when available."""
    18→    return which(name) or name
    19→
    20→
    21→def _selected_codes(category: str) -> list[str]:
    22→    select = []
    23→    if category in ("all", "imports"):
    24→        select.append("F401")
    25→    if category in ("all", "vars"):
    26→        select.append("F841")
    27→    return select
    28→
    29→
    30→def _is_excluded(filepath: str, exclusions: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
    31→    return bool(
    32→        exclusions
    33→        and any(_matches_exclusion(filepath, ex) for ex in exclusions)
    34→    )
    35→
    36→
    37→def _extract_unused_name(message: str, *, name_re: re.Pattern[str]) -> str:
    38→    match = name_re.search(message)
    39→    return match.group(1) if match else message.split()[0]
    40→
    41→
    42→def _parse_ruff_diagnostics(
    43→    diagnostics: list[dict],
    44→    *,
    45→    category: str,
    46→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...],
    47→) -> list[dict]:
    48→    entries = []
    49→    name_re = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
    50→    for diagnostic in diagnostics:
    51→        code = diagnostic.get("code", "")
    52→        filepath = diagnostic.get("filename", "")
    53→        if _is_excluded(filepath, exclusions):
    54→            continue
    55→
    56→        # F401 in __init__.py = re-export, not dead code
    57→        if code == "F401" and filepath.endswith("__init__.py"):
    58→            continue
    59→
    60→        cat = "imports" if code == "F401" else "vars"
    61→        if category != "all" and cat != category:
    62→            continue
    63→
    64→        message = diagnostic.get("message", "")
    65→        name = _extract_unused_name(message, name_re=name_re)
    66→        if name.startswith("_"):
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        location = diagnostic.get("location", {})
    70→        entries.append(
    71→            {
    72→                "file": filepath,
    73→                "line": location.get("row", 0),
    74→                "col": location.get("column", 0),
    75→                "name": name,
    76→                "category": cat,
    77→            }
    78→        )
    79→    return entries
    80→
    81→
    82→def _parse_pyflakes_lines(
    83→    lines: list[str],
    84→    *,
    85→    category: str,
    86→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...],
    87→) -> list[dict]:
    88→    entries = []
    89→    import_re = re.compile(r"^(.+):(\d+):\d*\s+'([^']+)'\s+imported but unused")
    90→    var_re = re.compile(
    91→        r"^(.+):(\d+):\d*\s+local variable '([^']+)' is assigned to but never used"
    92→    )
    93→
    94→    for line in lines:
    95→        import_match = import_re.match(line)
    96→        if import_match and category in ("all", "imports"):
    97→            filepath = import_match.group(1)
    98→            if _is_excluded(filepath, exclusions):
    99→                continue
   100→            # F401 in __init__.py = re-export, not dead code
   101→            if filepath.endswith("__init__.py"):
   102→                continue
   103→            entries.append(
   104→                {
   105→                    "file": filepath,
   106→                    "line": int(import_match.group(2)),
   107→                    "col": 0,
   108→                    "name": import_match.group(3),
   109→                    "category": "imports",
   110→                }
   111→            )
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        var_match = var_re.match(line)
   115→        if var_match and category in ("all", "vars"):
   116→            filepath = var_match.group(1)
   117→            if _is_excluded(filepath, exclusions):
   118→                continue
   119→            entries.append(
   120→                {
   121→                    "file": filepath,
   122→                    "line": int(var_match.group(2)),
   123→                    "col": 0,
   124→                    "name": var_match.group(3),
   125→                    "category": "vars",
   126→                }
   127→            )
   128→    return entries
   129→
   130→
   131→def detect_unused(path: Path, category: str = "all") -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   132→    """Detect unused imports and variables using ruff.
   133→
   134→    Falls back to pyflakes if ruff is not available.
   135→    Returns (entries, total_statements_checked).
   136→    """
   137→    total_files = len(find_py_files(path))
   138→
   139→    entries = _try_ruff(path, category)
   140→    if entries is not None:
   141→        return entries, total_files
   142→
   143→    entries = _try_pyflakes(path, category)
   144→    if entries is not None:
   145→        return entries, total_files
   146→
   147→    return [], total_files
   148→
   149→
   150→def _try_ruff(path: Path, category: str) -> list[dict] | None:
   151→    """Try ruff for unused detection."""
   152→    select = _selected_codes(category)
   153→    if not select:
   154→        return []
   155→
   156→    exclude_dirs = _collect_exclude_dirs(path)
   157→    cmd = [
   158→        "ruff",
   159→        "check",
   160→        "--select",
   161→        ",".join(select),
   162→        "--output-format",
   163→        "json",
   164→        "--no-fix",
   165→    ]
   166→    if exclude_dirs:
   167→        cmd.extend(["--exclude", ",".join(exclude_dirs)])
   168→    cmd.append(str(path))
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        # Static ruff argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
   172→        result = subprocess.run(
   173→            [_resolve_cli_executable(cmd[0]), *cmd[1:]],
   174→            capture_output=True,
   175→            text=True,
   176→            cwd=get_project_root(),
   177→            timeout=60,
   178→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
   179→        )
   180→    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   181→        return None
   182→
   183→    if not result.stdout.strip():
   184→        return []
   185→
   186→    try:
   187→        diagnostics = json.loads(result.stdout)
   188→    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   189→        return None
   190→
   191→    exclusions = _get_exclusions()
   192→    return _parse_ruff_diagnostics(
   193→        diagnostics, category=category, exclusions=exclusions
   194→    )
   195→
   196→
   197→def _try_pyflakes(path: Path, category: str) -> list[dict] | None:
   198→    """Fallback: try pyflakes for unused detection."""
   199→    try:
   200→        # Static pyflakes argv only; no shell expansion and executable path is resolved first.
   201→        result = subprocess.run(
   202→            [_resolve_cli_executable("pyflakes"), str(path)],
   203→            capture_output=True,
   204→            text=True,
   205→            cwd=get_project_root(),
   206→            timeout=60,
   207→            shell=False,  # nosec B603
   208→        )
   209→    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   210→        return None
   211→
   212→    exclusions = _get_exclusions()
   213→    lines = (result.stdout + result.stderr).splitlines()
   214→    return _parse_pyflakes_lines(lines, category=category, exclusions=exclusions)
   215→
```
