   223→def _config_hash_for_packet_contract(config: dict | None) -> str:
   224→    """Return a stable config hash used in prepared-packet reuse checks."""
   225→    redacted = redacted_review_config(config or {})
   226→    payload = json.dumps(redacted, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
   227→    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   228→
   229→
   230→def _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, *, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, object]:
   231→    """Build normalized invocation contract for prepared packet reuse."""
   232→    requested_dims = sorted(parse_dimensions(args) or set())
   233→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   234→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   235→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   236→        default=30,
   237→        minimum=1,
   238→    )
   239→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   240→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   241→        default=20,
   242→        minimum=1,
   243→    )
   244→    return {
   245→        "path": str(Path(getattr(args, "path", ".")).resolve()),
   246→        "dimensions": requested_dims,
   247→        "retrospective": retrospective,
   248→        "retrospective_max_issues": retrospective_max_issues,
   249→        "retrospective_max_batch_items": retrospective_max_batch_items,
   250→        "config_hash": _config_hash_for_packet_contract(config),
   251→    }
   252→
   253→
   254→def _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   255→    packet: dict[str, object],
   256→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   257→) -> str | None:
   258→    """Return mismatch reason for prepared packet reuse, else ``None``."""
   259→    raw_contract = packet.get(_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY)
   260→    if not isinstance(raw_contract, dict):
   261→        return "missing prepared packet contract metadata"
   262→
   263→    for key in (
   264→        "path",
   265→        "dimensions",
   266→        "retrospective",
   267→        "retrospective_max_issues",
   268→        "retrospective_max_batch_items",
   269→        "config_hash",
   270→    ):
   271→        if raw_contract.get(key) != expected_contract.get(key):
   272→            return f"contract field '{key}' differs"
   273→    return None
   274→
   275→
   276→def _try_load_prepared_packet(
   277→    *,
   278→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   279→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
   280→    """Load prepared packet from query.json when shape and contract match."""
   281→    try:
   282→        qf = query_file_path()
   283→        if not qf.exists():
   284→            return None, None
   285→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
   286→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, RuntimeError):
   287→        return None, "query.json is missing or invalid JSON"
   288→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   289→        return None, "prepared packet payload is not an object"
   290→    if "investigation_batches" not in data:
   291→        return None, "prepared packet is missing investigation batches"
   292→    batches = data["investigation_batches"]
   293→    if not isinstance(batches, list) or not batches:
   294→        return None, "prepared packet has no investigation batches"
   295→
   296→    mismatch_reason = _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   297→        data,
   298→        expected_contract,
   299→    )
   300→    if mismatch_reason is not None:
   301→        return None, mismatch_reason
   302→    return data, None
   303→
   304→
   305→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
   306→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
   307→    normalized_results: list[BatchResultPayload] = []
   308→    for result in batch_results:
   309→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
   310→            payload = result.to_dict()
   311→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
   312→                normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, payload))
   313→                continue
   314→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   315→            normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, result))
   316→    return merge_batch_results(
   317→        normalized_results,
   318→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   319→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def _load_or_prepare_packet(
   324→    args,
   325→    *,
   326→    state: dict,
   327→    lang,
   328→    config: dict,
   329→    stamp: str,
   330→) -> tuple[dict, Path, Path]:
   331→    """Load packet override or prepare a fresh packet snapshot."""
   332→    packet_override = getattr(args, "packet", None)
   333→    if packet_override:
   334→        packet_path = Path(packet_override)
   335→        if not packet_path.exists():
   336→            raise PacketValidationError(f"packet not found: {packet_override}", exit_code=1)
   337→        try:
   338→            packet = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   339→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   340→            raise PacketValidationError(f"reading packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   341→        blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   342→        blind_packet = build_blind_packet(packet)
   343→        safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   344→        print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   345→        print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_path}", "dim"))
   346→        return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   347→
   348→    # When no explicit --packet and no explicit --dimensions were given,
   349→    # check whether a prior ``review --prepare`` left a valid query.json
   350→    # packet we can reuse instead of rebuilding from scratch.
   351→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   352→
   353→    # Validate explicit dimensions against the language's scored dimensions.
   354→    if dims:
   355→        lang_obj = lang
   356→        lang_name = getattr(lang_obj, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang_obj, "lang", ""))
   357→        if lang_name:
   358→            valid_dims = set(scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name))
   359→            if valid_dims:
   360→                invalid = sorted(dims - valid_dims)
   361→                if invalid:
   362→                    valid_list = ", ".join(sorted(valid_dims))
   363→                    raise CommandError(
   364→                        f"Invalid dimensions for language '{lang_name}': {', '.join(invalid)}. "
   365→                        f"Valid dimensions: {valid_list}",
   366→                        exit_code=1,
   367→                    )
   368→
   369→    expected_contract = _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, config=config)
   370→    if not dims:
   371→        prepared, mismatch_reason = _try_load_prepared_packet(
   372→            expected_contract=expected_contract,
   373→        )
   374→        if prepared is not None:
   375→            print(colorize("  Reusing prepared packet from query.json", "dim"))
   376→            blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   377→            blind_packet = build_blind_packet(prepared)
   378→            safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   379→            packet_path, blind_saved = write_packet_snapshot(
   380→                prepared,
   381→                stamp=stamp,
   382→                review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   383→                blind_path=blind_path,
   384→                safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   385→            )
   386→            print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   387→            print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   388→            return prepared, packet_path, blind_saved
   389→        if mismatch_reason:
   390→            print(
   391→                colorize(
   392→                    f"  Prepared packet reuse rejected: {mismatch_reason}; rebuilding.",
   393→                    "dim",
   394→                )
   395→            )
   396→
   397→    path = Path(args.path)
   398→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   399→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   400→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   401→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   402→        default=30,
   403→        minimum=1,
   404→    )
   405→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   406→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   407→        default=20,
   408→        minimum=1,
   409→    )
   410→    lang_run, found_files = _setup_lang(lang, path, config)
   411→    lang_name = lang_run.name
   412→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   413→        state,
   414→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   418→    packet = prepare_holistic_review(
   419→        path,
   420→        lang_run,
   421→        state,
   422→        options=HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
   423→            dimensions=dimensions,
   424→            files=found_files or None,
   425→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
   426→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
   427→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   428→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   429→        ),
   430→    )
   431→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   432→    packet[_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY] = expected_contract
   433→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
   434→    next_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   435→    if retrospective:
   436→        next_command += (
   437→            " --retrospective"
   438→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
   439→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
   440→        )
   441→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   442→    write_query_best_effort(
   443→        packet,
   444→        context="review packet query update",
   445→    )
   446→    packet_path, blind_saved = write_packet_snapshot(
   447→        packet,
   448→        stamp=stamp,
   449→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   450→        blind_path=blind_path,
   451→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   452→    )
   453→    print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   454→    print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   455→    return packet, packet_path, blind_saved
   456→
   457→
   458→def do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   459→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   460→    from ..runtime.policy import resolve_batch_run_policy  # noqa: PLC0415
   461→
   462→    project_root = _runtime_project_root()
   463→    subagent_runs_dir = _subagent_runs_dir()
   464→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   465→    batch_deps = _build_batch_run_deps(
   466→        policy=policy,
   467→        project_root=project_root,
   468→    )
   469→    prepared = review_batch_phases_mod.prepare_batch_run(
   470→        args=args,
   471→        state=state,
   472→        lang=lang,
   473→        config=config or {},
   474→        deps=batch_deps,
   475→        project_root=project_root,
   476→        subagent_runs_dir=subagent_runs_dir,
   477→    )
   478→    if prepared is None:
   479→        return
   480→
   481→    executed = review_batch_phases_mod.execute_batch_run(
   482→        prepared=prepared,
   483→        deps=batch_deps,
   484→    )
   485→    review_batch_phases_mod.merge_and_import_batch_run(
   486→        prepared=prepared,
   487→        executed=executed,
   488→        state_file=state_file,
   489→        deps=batch_deps,
   490→    )
   491→
   492→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   493→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   494→
   495→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   496→    """
   497→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   498→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   499→
   500→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   501→    if not summary_path.exists():
   502→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   503→    try:
   504→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   505→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   506→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   507→
   508→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   509→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   510→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   511→
   512→    if not successful:
   513→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   514→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   515→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   516→
   517→    try:
   518→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   519→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   520→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   521→
   522→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   523→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   524→
   525→
   526→def do_import_run(
   527→    run_dir_path: str,
   528→    state: dict,
   529→    lang,
   530→    state_file: str,
   531→    *,
   532→    config: dict | None = None,
   533→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   534→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   535→    scan_path: str = ".",
   536→    dry_run: bool = False,
   537→) -> None:
   538→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   539→
   540→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   541→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   542→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   543→    """
   544→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   545→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   546→
   547→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   548→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   549→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   550→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   551→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   552→
   553→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   554→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   555→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   556→    output_files = {
   557→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   558→        for idx in selected_indexes
   559→    }
   560→
   561→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   562→    if missing:
   563→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   564→
   565→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   566→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   567→        failures=[],
   568→        output_files=output_files,
   569→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   570→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   571→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: normalize_batch_result(
   572→            payload,
   573→            dims,
   574→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   575→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   576→        ),
   577→    )
   578→
   579→    if not batch_results:
   580→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   581→
   582→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   583→    if failures:
   584→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   585→
   586→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   587→
   588→    # Reuse the canonical merge+metadata boundary from normal batch execution.
   589→    raw_batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   590→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   591→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   592→        raw_batches if isinstance(raw_batches, list) else [],
   593→        dimension_prompts=raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else None,
   594→    )
   595→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   596→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   597→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name) if lang_name else []
   598→    merged_path, missing_after_import = _merge_and_write_results(
   599→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   600→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_batch_import_provenance,
   601→        batch_results=batch_results,
   602→        batches=batches,
   603→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   604→        packet=packet,
   605→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   606→        scored_dimensions=scored_dimensions,
   607→        scan_path=scan_path,
   608→        runner=runner,
   609→        prompt_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   610→        stamp=stamp,
   611→        run_dir=run_dir,
   612→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   613→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   614→    )
   615→    _enforce_import_coverage(
   616→        missing_after_import=missing_after_import,
   617→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   618→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   619→        scan_path=scan_path,
   620→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   621→    )
   622→
   623→    # -- import with trusted source --
   624→    _do_import(
   625→        str(merged_path),
   626→        state,
   627→        lang,
   628→        state_file,
   629→        import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   630→            config=config,
   631→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   632→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   633→            trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   634→        ),
   635→        dry_run=dry_run,
   636→    )
   637→
   638→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   639→    if scan_after_import and not dry_run:
   640→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   641→        if lang_name:
   642→            run_followup_scan(
   643→                lang_name=lang_name,
   644→                scan_path=scan_path,
   645→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   646→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   647→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   648→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   649→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   650→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   651→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   652→                ),
   653→            )
   654→
```

1→"""Shared subprocess runner helpers for codex batch execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_process_impl.attempts import (
    10→    handle_early_attempt_return,
    11→    handle_failed_attempt,
    12→    handle_successful_attempt,
    13→    handle_timeout_or_stall,
    14→    resolve_retry_config,
    15→    run_batch_attempt,
    16→)

    23→
    24→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
    26→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    27→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
    28→        effort = "low"
    29→    return [
    30→        "codex",
    31→        "exec",
    32→        "--ephemeral",
    33→        "-C",
    34→        str(repo_root),
    35→        "-s",
    36→        "workspace-write",
    37→        "-c",
    38→        'approval_policy="never"',
    39→        "-c",
    40→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
    41→        "-o",
    42→        str(output_file),
    43→        prompt,
    44→    ]
    45→
    46→
    47→def run_codex_batch(
    48→    *,
    49→    prompt: str,
    50→    repo_root: Path,
    51→    output_file: Path,
    52→    log_file: Path,
    53→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    54→    codex_batch_command_fn=None,
    55→) -> int:
    56→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
    57→    if codex_batch_command_fn is None:
    58→        codex_batch_command_fn = codex_batch_command
    59→    cmd = codex_batch_command_fn(
    60→        prompt=prompt,
    61→        repo_root=repo_root,
    62→        output_file=output_file,
    63→    )
    64→    config = resolve_retry_config(deps)
    65→    log_sections: list[str] = []
    66→
    67→    for attempt in range(1, config.max_attempts + 1):
    68→        header, result = run_batch_attempt(
    69→            cmd=cmd,
    70→            deps=deps,
    71→            output_file=output_file,
    72→            log_file=log_file,
    73→            log_sections=log_sections,
    74→            attempt=attempt,
    75→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
    76→            use_popen=config.use_popen,
    77→            live_log_interval=config.live_log_interval,
    78→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    79→        )
    80→        early_return = handle_early_attempt_return(result)
    81→        if early_return is not None:
    82→            return early_return
    83→        timeout_or_stall = handle_timeout_or_stall(
    84→            header=header,
    85→            result=result,
    86→            deps=deps,
    87→            output_file=output_file,
    88→            log_file=log_file,
    89→            log_sections=log_sections,
    90→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    91→        )
    92→        if timeout_or_stall is not None:
    93→            if timeout_or_stall == 0:
    94→                return 0  # recovered from timeout/stall
    95→            # Non-recovered timeout/stall: retry if attempts remain
    96→            if attempt < config.max_attempts:
    97→                delay = config.retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))
    98→                log_sections.append(
    99→                    f"Timeout/stall on attempt {attempt}/{config.max_attempts}; "
   100→                    f"retrying in {delay:.1f}s."
   101→                )
   102→                if delay > 0:
   103→                    deps.sleep_fn(delay)
   104→                continue
   105→            return timeout_or_stall
   106→
   107→        log_sections.append(
   108→            f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
   109→        )
   110→
   111→        success_code = handle_successful_attempt(
   112→            result=result,
   113→            output_file=output_file,
   114→            log_file=log_file,
   115→            deps=deps,
   116→            log_sections=log_sections,
   117→        )
   118→        if success_code is not None:
   119→            return success_code
   120→        failure_code = handle_failed_attempt(
   121→            result=result,
   122→            deps=deps,
   123→            attempt=attempt,
   124→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
   125→            retry_backoff_seconds=config.retry_backoff_seconds,
   126→            log_file=log_file,
   127→            log_sections=log_sections,
   128→        )
   129→        if failure_code is not None:
   130→            return failure_code
   131→
   132→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   133→    return 1
   134→
   135→
   136→def run_followup_scan(
   137→    *,
   138→    lang_name: str,
   139→    scan_path: str,
   140→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   141→    force_queue_bypass: bool = False,
   142→) -> int:
   143→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   144→    scan_cmd = [
   145→        deps.python_executable,
   146→        "-m",
   147→        "desloppify",
   148→        "--lang",
   149→        lang_name,
   150→        "scan",
   151→        "--path",
   152→        scan_path,
   153→    ]
   154→    if force_queue_bypass:
   155→        followup_attest = (
   156→            "I understand this is not the intended workflow and "
   157→            "I am intentionally skipping queue completion"
   158→        )
   159→        scan_cmd.extend(["--force-rescan", "--attest", followup_attest])
   160→        print(
   161→            deps.colorize_fn(
   162→                "  Follow-up scan queue bypass enabled (--force-followup-scan).",
   163→                "yellow",
   164→            )
   165→        )
   166→    print(deps.colorize_fn("\n  Running follow-up scan...", "bold"))
   167→    try:
   168→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   169→            scan_cmd,
   170→            cwd=str(deps.project_root),
   171→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   172→        )
   173→    except deps.timeout_error:
   174→        print(
   175→            deps.colorize_fn(
   176→                f"  Follow-up scan timed out after {deps.timeout_seconds}s.",
   177→                "yellow",
   178→            ),
   179→            file=sys.stderr,
   180→        )
   181→        return 124
   182→    except OSError as exc:
   183→        print(
   184→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   185→            file=sys.stderr,
   186→        )
   187→        return 1
   188→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)
   189→
   190→
   191→__all__ = [
   192→    "CodexBatchRunnerDeps",
   193→    "FollowupScanDeps",
   194→    "extract_payload_from_log",
   195→    "codex_batch_command",
   196→    "run_codex_batch",
   197→    "run_followup_scan",
   198→]
   199→

    34→@contextmanager
    35→def _managed_live_writer(
    36→    state: _RunnerState,
    37→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
    38→    interval: float,
    39→):
    40→    """Start/stop the live-writer thread around one runner attempt."""
    41→    writer_thread = _start_live_writer(state, ctx, interval)
    42→    try:
    43→        yield
    44→    finally:
    45→        state.stop_event.set()
    46→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)

    48→
    49→def _runner_error_result(
    50→    *,
    51→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
    52→    heading: str,
    53→    exc: Exception,
    54→    exit_code: int,
    55→) -> _ExecutionResult:
    56→    """Build a consistent error result for runner invocation failures."""
    57→    ctx.log_sections.append(f"{ctx.header}\n\n{heading}:\n{exc}\n")
    58→    ctx.safe_write_text_fn(ctx.log_file, "\n\n".join(ctx.log_sections))
    59→    return _ExecutionResult(
    60→        code=exit_code,
    61→        stdout_text="",
    62→        stderr_text="",
    63→        early_return=exit_code,
    64→    )

    67→def _run_via_popen(
    68→    cmd: list[str],
    69→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    70→    state: _RunnerState,

    72→    interval: float,

    92→            RuntimeError,
    93→            ValueError,
    94→            TypeError,
    95→            subprocess.SubprocessError,
    96→        ) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
    97→            return _runner_error_result(
    98→                ctx=ctx,
    99→                heading="UNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR",
   100→                exc=exc,
   101→                exit_code=1,
   102→            )
   103→
   104→        stdout_thread = threading.Thread(
   105→            target=_drain_stream,
   106→            args=(process.stdout, state.stdout_chunks, state),
   107→            daemon=True,
   108→        )
   109→        stderr_thread = threading.Thread(
   110→            target=_drain_stream,
   111→            args=(process.stderr, state.stderr_chunks, state),
   112→            daemon=True,
   113→        )
   114→        stdout_thread.start()
   115→        stderr_thread.start()
   116→
   117→        timed_out = False
   118→        stalled = False
   119→        recovered_from_stall = False
   120→        output_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = None
   121→        output_stable_since: float | None = None
   122→
   123→        while process.poll() is None:
   124→            now_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   125→            elapsed = int(max(0.0, now_monotonic - ctx.started_monotonic))
   126→            if elapsed >= deps.timeout_seconds:
   127→                with state.lock:
   128→                    state.runner_note = f"timeout after {deps.timeout_seconds}s"
   129→                timed_out = True
   130→                _terminate_process(process)
   131→                break
   132→            if stall_seconds > 0:
   133→                with state.lock:
   134→                    last_activity = state.last_stream_activity
   135→                stalled, output_signature, output_stable_since = _check_stall(
   136→                    ctx.output_file,
   137→                    output_signature,
   138→                    output_stable_since,
   139→                    now_monotonic,
   140→                    last_activity,
   141→                    stall_seconds,
   142→                )
   143→                if stalled:
   144→                    with state.lock:
   145→                        state.runner_note = (
   146→                            f"stall recovery triggered after {stall_seconds}s "
   147→                            "with stable output state"
   148→                        )
   149→                    recovered_from_stall = _output_file_has_json_payload(ctx.output_file)
   150→                    _terminate_process(process)
   151→                    break
   152→            deps.sleep_fn(min(interval, 1.0))
   153→
   154→        if process.poll() is None:
   155→            _terminate_process(process)
   156→        stdout_thread.join(timeout=2)
   157→        stderr_thread.join(timeout=2)
   158→        _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   159→        return _ExecutionResult(
   160→            code=int(process.returncode or 0),
   161→            stdout_text="".join(state.stdout_chunks),
   162→            stderr_text="".join(state.stderr_chunks),
   163→            timed_out=timed_out,
   164→            stalled=stalled,
   165→            recovered_from_stall=recovered_from_stall,
   166→        )
   167→
   168→
   169→def _run_via_subprocess(
   170→    cmd: list[str],
   171→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   172→    state: _RunnerState,
   173→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   174→    interval: float,
   175→) -> _ExecutionResult:
   176→    with _managed_live_writer(state, ctx, interval):
   177→        try:
   178→            result = deps.subprocess_run(
   179→                cmd,
   180→                capture_output=True,
   181→                text=True,
   182→                timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   183→            )
   184→        except deps.timeout_error:
   185→            return _ExecutionResult(code=124, stdout_text="", stderr_text="", timed_out=True)
   186→        except OSError as exc:
   187→            return _runner_error_result(
   188→                ctx=ctx,
   189→                heading="RUNNER ERROR",
   190→                exc=exc,
   191→                exit_code=127,
   192→            )
   193→        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   194→            return _runner_error_result(
   195→                ctx=ctx,
   196→                heading="UNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR",
   197→                exc=exc,
   198→                exit_code=1,
   199→            )
   200→
   201→        return _ExecutionResult(
   202→            code=int(result.returncode),
   203→            stdout_text=result.stdout or "",
   204→            stderr_text=result.stderr or "",
   205→        )
   206→
   207→
   208→def resolve_retry_config(deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps) -> _RetryConfig:
   209→    retries_raw = deps.max_retries if isinstance(deps.max_retries, int) else 0
   210→    max_retries = max(0, retries_raw)
   211→    max_attempts = max_retries + 1
   212→    backoff_raw = (
   213→        float(deps.retry_backoff_seconds)
   214→        if isinstance(deps.retry_backoff_seconds, int | float)
   215→        else 0.0
   216→    )
   217→    retry_backoff_seconds = max(0.0, backoff_raw)
   218→    live_log_interval = (
   219→        float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds)
   220→        if isinstance(deps.live_log_interval_seconds, int | float)
   221→        and float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds) > 0
   222→        else 5.0
   223→    )
   224→    stall_seconds = (
   225→        int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds)
   226→        if isinstance(deps.stall_after_output_seconds, int | float)
   227→        and int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds) > 0
   228→        else 0
   229→    )
   230→    use_popen = bool(deps.use_popen_runner) and callable(
   231→        getattr(deps, "subprocess_popen", None)
   232→    )
   233→    return _RetryConfig(
   234→        max_attempts=max_attempts,
   235→        retry_backoff_seconds=retry_backoff_seconds,
   236→        live_log_interval=live_log_interval,
   237→        stall_seconds=stall_seconds,
   238→        use_popen=use_popen,
   239→    )
   240→
   241→
   242→def run_batch_attempt(
   243→    *,
   244→    cmd: list[str],
   245→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   246→    output_file: Path,
   247→    log_file: Path,
   248→    log_sections: list[str],
   249→    attempt: int,
   250→    max_attempts: int,
   251→    use_popen: bool,
   252→    live_log_interval: float,
   253→    stall_seconds: int,
   254→) -> tuple[str, _ExecutionResult]:
   255→    header = f"ATTEMPT {attempt}/{max_attempts}\n$ {' '.join(cmd)}"
   256→    started_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   257→    state = _RunnerState(last_stream_activity=started_monotonic)
   258→    ctx = _AttemptContext(
   259→        header=header,
   260→        started_at_iso=datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   261→        started_monotonic=started_monotonic,
   262→        output_file=output_file,
   263→        log_file=log_file,
   264→        log_sections=log_sections,
   265→        safe_write_text_fn=deps.safe_write_text_fn,
   266→    )
   267→    _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   268→    if use_popen:
   269→        result = _run_via_popen(
   270→            cmd,
   271→            deps,
   272→            state,
   273→            ctx,
   274→            live_log_interval,
   275→            stall_seconds,
   276→        )
   277→    else:
   278→        result = _run_via_subprocess(cmd, deps, state, ctx, live_log_interval)
   279→    return header, result
   280→
   281→
   282→def handle_early_attempt_return(result: _ExecutionResult) -> int | None:
   283→    return result.early_return
   284→
   285→
   286→def handle_timeout_or_stall(
   287→    *,
   288→    header: str,
   289→    result: _ExecutionResult,
   290→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   291→    output_file: Path,
   292→    log_file: Path,
   293→    log_sections: list[str],
   294→    stall_seconds: int,
   295→) -> int | None:
   296→    if not result.timed_out and not result.stalled:
   297→        return None
   298→    if result.timed_out:
   299→        log_sections.append(
   300→            f"{header}\n\nTIMEOUT after {deps.timeout_seconds}s\n\n"
   301→            f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
   302→        )
   303→    else:
   304→        log_sections.append(
   305→            f"{header}\n\nSTALL RECOVERY after {stall_seconds}s "
   306→            "of stable output and no stream activity.\n\n"
   307→            f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
   308→        )
   309→    if _output_file_has_json_payload(output_file):
   310→        recovery_message = (
   311→            "Recovered timed-out batch from JSON output file; "
   312→            "continuing as success."
   313→            if result.timed_out
   314→            else "Recovered stalled batch from JSON output file; "
   315→            "continuing as success."
   316→        )
   317→        log_sections.append(recovery_message)
   318→        deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   319→        return 0
   320→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   321→    return 124
   322→
   323→
   324→def handle_successful_attempt(
   325→    *,
   326→    result: _ExecutionResult,
   327→    output_file: Path,
   328→    log_file: Path,
   329→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   330→    log_sections: list[str],
   331→) -> int | None:
   332→    if result.code != 0:
   333→        return None
   334→    if not output_file.exists():
   335→        log_sections.append("Runner returned 0 but output file is missing.")
   336→    validate_fn = deps.validate_output_fn
   337→    if validate_fn is None:
   338→        validate_fn = _output_file_has_json_payload
   339→    return handle_successful_attempt_core(
   340→        result=result,
   341→        output_file=output_file,
   342→        log_file=log_file,
   343→        deps=deps,
   344→        log_sections=log_sections,
   345→        default_validate_fn=validate_fn,
   346→        monotonic_fn=time.monotonic,
   347→    )
   348→
   349→
   350→def handle_failed_attempt(
   351→    *,
   352→    result: _ExecutionResult,
   353→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   354→    attempt: int,
   355→    max_attempts: int,
   356→    retry_backoff_seconds: float,
   357→    log_file: Path,
   358→    log_sections: list[str],
   359→) -> int | None:
   360→    combined = f"{result.stdout_text}\n{result.stderr_text}".lower()
   361→    is_transient = any(needle in combined for needle in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES)
   362→    if not is_transient or attempt >= max_attempts:
   363→        deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   364→        return result.code
   365→    delay_seconds = retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))
   366→    log_sections.append(
   367→        "Transient runner failure detected; "
   368→        f"retrying in {delay_seconds:.1f}s (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_attempts})."
   369→    )
   370→    try:
   371→        if delay_seconds > 0:
   372→            deps.sleep_fn(delay_seconds)
   373→    except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   374→        log_sections.append(
   375→            f"Retry delay hook failed: {exc} — aborting remaining retries."
   376→        )
   377→        deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   378→        return 1
   379→    return None
   380→
   381→
   382→__all__ = [
   383→    "handle_early_attempt_return",
   384→    "handle_failed_attempt",
   385→    "handle_successful_attempt",
   386→    "handle_timeout_or_stall",
   387→    "resolve_retry_config",
   388→    "run_batch_attempt",
   389→    "_run_via_popen",
   390→    "_run_via_subprocess",
   391→]
   392→

1→"""I/O and log helpers for review batch process execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import subprocess  # nosec
     8→import threading
     9→import time
    10→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    11→from pathlib import Path
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    14→
    15→from .types import _AttemptContext, _RunnerState
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _output_file_status_text(output_file: Path) -> str:
    21→    """Describe output file state for live log snapshots."""
    22→    if not output_file.exists():
    23→        return f"{output_file} (missing)"
    24→    try:
    25→        stat = output_file.stat()
    26→    except OSError as exc:
    27→        return f"{output_file} (exists; stat failed: {exc})"
    28→    modified_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime, tz=UTC).isoformat(
    29→        timespec="seconds"
    30→    )
    31→    return f"{output_file} (exists; bytes={stat.st_size}; modified={modified_at})"
    32→
    33→
    34→def _output_file_has_json_payload(output_file: Path) -> bool:
    35→    """Return True when the output file contains a valid JSON object."""
    36→    if not output_file.exists():
    37→        return False
    38→    try:
    39→        payload = json.loads(output_file.read_text())
    40→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
    41→        return False
    42→    return isinstance(payload, dict)
    43→
    44→
    45→def extract_payload_from_log(
    46→    batch_index: int,
    47→    raw_path: Path,
    48→    extract_fn,
    49→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
    50→    """Try to recover a batch payload from the runner log file."""
    51→    log_path = raw_path.parent.parent / "logs" / f"batch-{batch_index + 1}.log"
    52→    if not log_path.exists():
    53→        return None
    54→    try:
    55→        log_text = log_path.read_text()
    56→    except OSError:
    57→        return None
    58→
    59→    stdout_marker = "\nSTDOUT:\n"
    60→    stderr_marker = "\n\nSTDERR:\n"
    61→    stdout_start = log_text.rfind(stdout_marker)
    62→    if stdout_start == -1 and log_text.startswith("STDOUT:\n"):
    63→        stdout_start = 0
    64→        stdout_offset = len("STDOUT:\n")
    65→    elif stdout_start >= 0:
    66→        stdout_offset = len(stdout_marker)
    67→    else:
    68→        stdout_offset = 0
    69→    if stdout_start >= 0:
    70→        start_idx = stdout_start + stdout_offset
    71→        stdout_end = log_text.find(stderr_marker, start_idx)
    72→        stdout_text = (
    73→            log_text[start_idx:] if stdout_end == -1 else log_text[start_idx:stdout_end]
    74→        )
    75→        payload = extract_fn(stdout_text)
    76→        if payload is not None:
    77→            return payload
    78→        # If the batch log has a concrete STDOUT section but it contains no parseable
    79→        # payload, do not fallback to parsing the whole log. Full logs include the
    80→        # prompt template (often with JSON examples), which can hide true STDERR failures.
    81→        return None
    82→
    83→    return extract_fn(log_text)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _terminate_process(process: subprocess.Popen[str]) -> None:
    87→    """Terminate (then kill) a subprocess that may still be running."""
    88→    if process.poll() is not None:
    89→        return
    90→    try:
    91→        process.terminate()
    92→        process.wait(timeout=3)
    93→        return
    94→    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
    95→        log_best_effort_failure(
    96→            logger,
    97→            "terminate review subprocess before kill fallback",
    98→            exc,
    99→        )
   100→    try:
   101→        process.kill()
   102→        process.wait(timeout=3)
   103→    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
   104→        return
   105→
   106→
   107→def _drain_stream(stream, sink: list[str], state: _RunnerState) -> None:
   108→    """Read lines from *stream* into *sink*, updating activity timestamp."""
   109→    if stream is None:
   110→        return
   111→    try:
   112→        for chunk in iter(stream.readline, ""):
   113→            if not chunk:
   114→                break
   115→            with state.lock:
   116→                sink.append(chunk)
   117→                state.last_stream_activity = time.monotonic()
   118→    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   119→        with state.lock:
   120→            sink.append(f"\n[stream read error: {exc}]\n")
   121→    finally:
   122→        try:
   123→            stream.close()
   124→        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
   125→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "close review batch stream", exc)
   126→
   127→
   128→def _write_live_snapshot(state: _RunnerState, ctx: _AttemptContext) -> None:
   129→    """Write a point-in-time log snapshot while the runner is active."""
   130→    elapsed_seconds = int(max(0.0, time.monotonic() - ctx.started_monotonic))
   131→    with state.lock:
   132→        stdout_preview = "".join(state.stdout_chunks)
   133→        stderr_preview = "".join(state.stderr_chunks)
   134→        note = state.runner_note
   135→    note_block = f"\nRUNNER NOTE: {note}" if note else ""
   136→    ctx.safe_write_text_fn(
   137→        ctx.log_file,
   138→        "\n\n".join(
   139→            ctx.log_sections
   140→            + [
   141→                (
   142→                    f"{ctx.header}\n\n"
   143→                    "STATUS: running\n"
   144→                    f"STARTED AT: {ctx.started_at_iso}\n"
   145→                    f"ELAPSED: {elapsed_seconds}s\n"
   146→                    f"OUTPUT FILE: {_output_file_status_text(ctx.output_file)}"
   147→                    f"{note_block}\n\n"
   148→                    f"STDOUT (live):\n{stdout_preview}\n\n"
   149→                    f"STDERR (live):\n{stderr_preview}\n"
   150→                )
   151→            ]
   152→        ),
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def _start_live_writer(
   157→    state: _RunnerState,
   158→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   159→    interval: float,
   160→) -> threading.Thread:
   161→    """Spawn a daemon thread that periodically writes live log snapshots."""
   162→
   163→    def _loop() -> None:
   164→        while not state.stop_event.wait(interval):
   165→            _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   166→
   167→    thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True)
   168→    thread.start()
   169→    return thread
   170→
   171→
   172→def _check_stall(
   173→    output_file: Path,
   174→    prev_sig: tuple[int, int] | None,
   175→    prev_stable: float | None,
   176→    now: float,
   177→    last_activity: float,
   178→    threshold: int,
   179→) -> tuple[bool, tuple[int, int] | None, float | None]:
   180→    """Check for runner stall. Returns (stalled, new_sig, new_stable_since).
   181→
   182→    When no output artifact exists yet the batch is still initialising — the
   183→    stall detector must not declare a stall.  The caller already enforces the
   184→    real timeout, so batches that never produce output will still be killed;
   185→    they just won't be killed *early* by the stall heuristic.
   186→    """
   187→    try:
   188→        stat = output_file.stat()
   189→        current_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = (
   190→            int(stat.st_size),
   191→            int(stat.st_mtime),
   192→        )
   193→    except OSError:
   194→        current_signature = None
   195→    if current_signature is None:
   196→        # No output file yet — the batch hasn't started writing.  Never
   197→        # declare a stall in this state; just carry forward the baseline
   198→        # timestamp so that once the file *does* appear, we start fresh.
   199→        baseline = prev_stable if isinstance(prev_stable, int | float) else now
   200→        return False, None, baseline
   201→    if current_signature != prev_sig:
   202→        return False, current_signature, now
   203→    if prev_stable is None:
   204→        return False, prev_sig, prev_stable
   205→    output_age = now - prev_stable
   206→    stream_idle = now - last_activity
   207→    if output_age >= threshold and stream_idle >= threshold:
   208→        return True, prev_sig, prev_stable
   209→    return False, prev_sig, prev_stable
   210→
   211→
   212→__all__ = [
   213→    "_check_stall",
   214→    "_drain_stream",
   215→    "extract_payload_from_log",
   216→    "_output_file_has_json_payload",
   217→    "_output_file_status_text",
   218→    "_start_live_writer",
   219→    "_terminate_process",
   220→    "_write_live_snapshot",
   221→]
   222→
```

The command is built in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/runner/codex_batch.py` in the `codex_batch_command()` function (lines 24-44):

```python
def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
        effort = "low"
    return [
        "codex",
        "exec",
        "--ephemeral",
        "-C", str(repo_root),
        "-s", "workspace-write",
        "-c", 'approval_policy="never"',
        "-c", f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
        "-o", str(output_file),
        prompt,
    ]
```

**Key parameters:**
- Reads `DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT` environment variable (defaults to "low")
- Validates effort level to one of: low, medium, high, xhigh
- Changes to repo_root directory with `-C`
- Grants workspace-write scope with `-s`
- Sets approval_policy to "never"
- Outputs results to specified file with `-o`

def _build_codex_batch_runner_deps(policy) -> CodexBatchRunnerDeps:

**Retry flow** (attempts.py, lines 67-105):
- Attempts up to `max_retries + 1` times
- On failure, checks stderr/stdout for transient error markers (runner_failures.py)
- Exponential backoff: `delay = retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))`
